### From brief to shipping.
Agree the scope, price and responsible parties before each paid stage.
- 1

#### Brief
You + Packrivo
Share product dimensions, quantity per design, artwork and destination.
- 2

#### Supplier matching
Packrivo + Supplier
Check structure, materials, quantity and the supplier’s proposed scope.
- 3

#### Quotation
Supplier + Packrivo + You
Compare itemized terms and approve the commercial scope.
- 4

#### Sample
Supplier + You
Make and review the agreed sample; record changes and approval.
- 5

#### Production & inspection
Supplier + Packrivo + You
Produce to the approved specification; agree inspection scope and release responsibility.
- 6

#### Shipping
Supplier / logistics provider + You
Confirm packed dimensions, delivery terms and destination requirements.

### Questions before you request a quote.
What does Packrivo do?

Packrivo is a Shenzhen-based packaging sourcing and project coordination service. We help small brands prepare specifications, compare supplier quotations and review samples. Independent suppliers handle manufacturing.What is the minimum order?

Our starting quantity is 500 pieces per product / design. State the quantity for each design so material, construction and artwork requirements can be quoted together.How are suppliers selected?

We compare the project requirements with the supplier’s proposed structure, materials, quantity and sample scope. Ask which checks have been completed for your selected supplier before ordering.How are project costs calculated?

Coordination fees range from 8–15%. The quotation identifies the fee basis and included work alongside supplier pricing, sampling, freight and other applicable costs.How long do samples and production take?

Share your target date and destination. The schedule depends on materials, artwork, sample approval and quantity, so the responsible supplier confirms the project timeline in the quotation.Are these pictures physical samples?

The site uses clearly labelled concept renders to explain packaging options. Physical samples are used to review actual fit, materials and finish for your project.

## About Packrivo: Packaging Sourcing in Shenzhen, China

URL: https://www.packrivo.com/about/

SHENZHEN · CHINA

## About Packrivo
Packaging sourcing and project coordination for small jewelry, candle and gift brands.
We help you define packaging specifications, compare independent supplier quotations and review samples. Independent suppliers manufacture the packaging; the project quotation identifies the contracting seller and agreed scope.

### Working with Packrivo at a glance

#### What we source
Jewelry boxes, gift boxes, mailer boxes, labels, inserts and bags & pouches for small jewelry, candle and gift brands.

#### Order size
MOQ 500 pieces per product/design. Quantities, sample scope and pricing are confirmed in the project quotation.

#### How we charge
A coordination fee of 8–15%. The calculation basis and included work are agreed in writing before paid work starts. See the service scope.

#### How to reach us
In English by email or WhatsApp, from Shenzhen, China (UTC+8).

### What to confirm before ordering
Check the proposed materials, sample scope, itemized quotation, delivery terms and who approves production. Read the service scope and responsibilities before starting.

### How we prepare our guides
Packrivo publishes the journal as an organization. AI tools assist with drafting, editing and concept diagrams. Cited sources and labeled examples support purchasing questions; illustrative examples are not records of customer orders.
Send corrections with the page link to [email protected].

## Custom Clear Display Box Sourcing

URL: https://www.packrivo.com/clear-display-box/

AI-assisted packaging concept. Review the actual materials and fit on a physical sample.

Foldable gift boxes

## Custom Clear Display Box Sourcing
MOQ: 500 pieces per product / design. Plan the quantity for each design in your brief.
A clear display pack makes some of the product visible through a transparent shell. The exact construction could be folded, hinged or thermoformed, so specify the intended form.
PET is one possible material option for this concept, not a supplier-confirmed resin or grade.
Packaging option · Supplier confirmation required

### Quote Details
Send the product dimensions, quantity by design and destination. Include the structure, material and artwork preferences you already know; flag open questions so supplier quotes use the same scope.Get a QuoteExplore customization →

### Product specifications
Minimum orderMOQ 500 pieces per design.
Get a QuoteShare your dimensions, quantity and delivery destination.
The minimum is Packrivo’s project threshold. Availability for the chosen structure, material and finish is checked for your project.

### What affects the price
-

#### Size
The finished dimensions and usable interior determine material use and product fit.
-

#### Structure
Closures, folds and assembly steps change the work needed to make the pack.
-

#### Materials
Board, wrap and insert choices affect both construction and material cost.
-

#### Printing & finishes
Artwork coverage, colors and decoration steps change setup and finishing costs.
-

#### Quantity
The quantity per design changes how setup and production costs are distributed.

### When it may fit
It may suit retail display when visibility is important. It may not fit a buyer who needs full opacity or a paper-only package.

### Details to define

- Product shape, dimensions, weight and display side.

- Clear shell format, opening, closure and wall thickness request.

- Insert, paper sleeve, hanger or graphic support.

- Destination market, product contact and documentation needed.

### Artwork and presentation choices
Use the backing card or proposed printed area to locate your artwork. Review visibility through the clear shell with the complete contents, then record which surfaces need handling observations.

### Options to compare

- Compare a clear clamshell with a paperboard window carton.

- Add a bright printed card behind the clear shell for color and branding.

- Keep scratch, static and contact questions open for supplier review.

### Supplier confirmation needed
Confirm exact resin, forming method, closure, surface handling and market requirements. Do not infer recyclability, food-contact compliance or protection from appearance.

### Project details to agree
Final materials, construction, price, sample scope and delivery timing are confirmed for the agreed project with the relevant supplier.

### Frequently asked questionsDoes a transparent box tell me which plastic it uses?No. Ask the supplier for the specific resin and product documentation for the intended market.Is the material or use claim confirmed?No. This is an inquiry candidate. Exact composition, market suitability, supplier feasibility, project price and timing and performance need evidence.

### Request a Quote
Compare with Packrivo packaging options and Get a Quote. This is a local review draft only.

## Custom Packaging Suppliers Compared for Small Brands

URL: https://www.packrivo.com/compare/

### Comparison table

|
| Minimum order
| Production time
| Best for

| Packlane
| Low minimums; no fixed number published
| Most custom packaging 7–15 business days, plus shipping; rush on select products
| Small first runs of mailers and folding cartons with instant online pricing

| Arka
| Branded mailers: 1 sample, then production from 10 units; custom cartons 250 units
| Standard 7–10 business days, rush 3–5 business days, after proof approval
| Very small branded mailer runs and fast standard production

| Packhelp
| Custom-size mailers 100 pieces (plus a die-cut fee from €240 net on the first order); custom-size product boxes 1,000 pieces
| Set per order for custom sizes; standard sizes ship faster
| Brands in Europe ordering printed mailers with EU delivery

| PakFactory
| Set per specification; no fixed minimum published
| Rigid boxes 25–30 business days; folding cartons 15–20 business days, after final proof
| Managed custom packaging projects with a dedicated product specialist

| Packrivo
| 500 pieces per design
| Stock jewelry boxes 1–15 days; custom printed boxes about 5–30 days after approval
| Custom jewelry, candle and gift packaging at 500+ pieces per design

### Detailed comparisons

- Packlane vs Packrivo

- Arka vs Packrivo

- Packhelp vs Packrivo

- PakFactory vs Packrivo

### Frequently asked questions

#### Which custom packaging supplier has the lowest minimum order?

Arka lists branded mailer production from 10 units and Packlane advertises low minimums, so both suit very small runs. Packhelp requires 100 pieces for custom-size mailers. Packrivo projects start at 500 pieces per design.

#### Which option is best for custom jewelry boxes?

Rigid jewelry boxes such as drawer, magnetic flip-lid and book-style boxes are usually made by specialist factories. Packrivo compares quotations from independent factories in China for these boxes from 500 pieces per design.

### Sources

Supplier terms were checked on their own pages on October 22, 2026. Terms change; confirm current minimums, prices and timing with each supplier before ordering.

- Packlane homepage and FAQ

- Arka FAQ

- Packhelp help centre: custom box sizes

- PakFactory support: turnaround times

## Gift Boxes & Retail Packaging
MOQ: 500 pieces per product / design. Plan the quantity for each design in your brief.
Compare folding and rigid gift boxes, bags, sleeves and retail displays. Define the contents, opening and delivery state before requesting a quote.
Candles, gifts and small retail products.Get a Quote
AI-assisted concept visualization. Not a physical sample.

### Compare gift box construction and delivery state
Name the structure as well as the supplied state. A magnetic opening and a flat shipment are separate decisions. Compare flat and assembled delivery responsibilities before comparing proposals.
On a small screen, each direction is grouped with its inputs and sample question.

Planning directions. Confirm the proposed construction and fit through supplier review and an appropriate sample.
| Direction | Use and inputs | Sample question
| Folding carton |
Consider a paperboard carton for a retail presentation brief.
Bring: Formed interior, product weight, fold pattern, closure and insert. | Can the sample be assembled and closed with the complete product?
| Tuck-end carton |
Consider an end-flap closure when its loading route suits the product.
Bring: Loading end, product envelope, closure and artwork panels. | Do the end flaps close as intended after loading?
| Collapsible magnetic box |
Consider a collapsible presentation structure when flat supply is required.
Bring: Supplied state, assembly steps, consumables and finished dimensions. | Which parts fold, and what work remains at your packing bench?
| Windowed carton |
Consider a visible-product area when it serves a display need.
Bring: Window position, proposed window material, attachment and product position. | Is the intended detail visible, with the proposed window and carton in place?

All options require supplier feasibility confirmation. Category imagery illustrates a concept, not every listed construction.

### What to include
Formed internal size; product weight; closure; assembly state; board direction; insert.

### What to check
Confirm that the selected construction really ships flat. Test load, assembly and crease quality.Explore customization →

### Plan the complete gift
For a candle, brief the box, insert and outer pack as separate components. If the gift also needs a bag and tissue, prepare the bag and tissue specifications rather than assuming the box quote includes them.

### Product specifications
Minimum orderMOQ 500 pieces per design.
Get a QuoteShare your dimensions, quantity and delivery destination.
The minimum is Packrivo’s project threshold. Availability for the chosen structure, material and finish is checked for your project.

### What affects the price
-

#### Size
The finished dimensions and usable interior determine material use and product fit.
-

#### Structure
Closures, folds and assembly steps change the work needed to make the pack.
-

#### Materials
Board, wrap and insert choices affect both construction and material cost.
-

#### Printing & finishes
Artwork coverage, colors and decoration steps change setup and finishing costs.
-

#### Quantity
The quantity per design changes how setup and production costs are distributed.

### When it may fit
It may suit retail gifting, events or a customer handover. The set does not establish parcel protection on its own.

### Details to define

- Gift or box dimensions, weight and carry use.

- Bag size, gusset, handle type and print area.

- Tissue size, fold, color/pattern direction and quantity.

- Destination, per-component quantities and whether a box or label is included.

### Artwork and presentation choices
Give the bag and tissue their own color and artwork references. Describe whether they should match or coordinate, and retain separate quantities and file versions for the two components.

### Options to compare

- Try contrasting color families, graphic patterns or textured uncoated papers.

- Decide whether the bag and tissue should match exactly or simply coordinate.

- List each component separately for later quote comparison.

### Supplier confirmation needed
Confirm paper and handle specifications, printing, color sample, quantities and whether one supplier can provide the set. No bundle availability or sustainability claim is implied.

### Project details to agree
Final materials, construction, price, sample scope and delivery timing are confirmed for the agreed project with the relevant supplier.

### Frequently asked questionsCan the bag and tissue use different colors?Yes, a buyer can specify a coordinated palette. Ask what proof or sample can be reviewed before approving production colors.Is the material, supplier or performance confirmed?No. This is an inquiry candidate. Material specifications, supplier feasibility, project price and timing and performance need project-specific evidence.

### Request a Quote
Compare with Packrivo packaging options and Get a Quote. This local page draft does not create a commercial offer.

## Check whether a gift box really folds flat

URL: https://www.packrivo.com/guide-gift-box-flat-pack-check/

Ask for two views: the supplied flat state and the assembled box. A magnetic lid or the words “gift box” alone do not establish a collapsible construction.

### Identify the construction
Separate folding paperboard cartons from rigid collapsible boxes and fixed rigid boxes. Ask which panels fold, how the sides lock and whether adhesive is applied by the supplier or during your own packing process.

### Check the assembly task
Request assembly instructions and test them with a physical sample. Note whether a person needs tools, glue, peeling strips or extra parts. Confirm if the box can be reopened or flattened again; some constructions are intended for one-time assembly.

### Ask for the actual shipping pack
Request units per shipping carton, carton external dimensions, gross weight and any protective wrapping. Flat delivery may reduce space, but freight savings cannot be calculated from a render or assumed across suppliers and routes.

### Test the gift inside
Place the intended product and insert into the formed box. Check base support, sidewall stiffness and closure clearance. For glass candle jars, presentation packaging and parcel protection are separate questions that need to be considered together.

### Use this comparison checklistConstruction and closure:
Supplied state: flat / assembled / partly assembled
Assembly steps and consumables:
Product weight and formed internal size:
Units per shipping carton:
Carton dimensions and gross weight:
Sample result and unresolved checks:

Do not approve a “premium” or “eco” description as a specification. Ask for the actual material, construction and evidence supporting any environmental claim.

### Put the checklist to work.
Bring the details you have. Mark anything else as unknown.Get a QuoteExplore related packaging →

## Jewelry Insert Fit Working Sheet

URL: https://www.packrivo.com/guide-jewelry-insert-fit/

Use this sheet to collect jewelry geometry, contact restrictions, removal access and the matching box reference in one record. It produces a supplier question list; it does not choose a universal cutout allowance.

### Fill the record with your own product references
Enter one line for each complete piece, including backs, clasps and loose chain. Write unknown where information is missing. Keep the original units beside a measurement and identify the drawing or item that it belongs to.
This working field stays in the current page; it does not submit an inquiry. Copy your completed notes before leaving. You can also print this page using your browser.Your jewelry insert recordPROJECT / DATE / RECORD OWNER:
PRODUCT REFERENCE AND VERSION:
JEWELRY PIECE 1 — overall envelope / units:
JEWELRY PIECE 1 — posts, clasp or other projections:
JEWELRY PIECE 2 — overall envelope / units:
JEWELRY PIECE 2 — posts, backs or other projections:
CHAIN ROUTE / LOOSE PARTS:

CONTACT ALLOWED / CONTACT TO REVIEW:
CUSTOMER LIFT POINT AND REMOVAL ORDER:

HOLDER / PROPOSED MATERIAL / DRAWING VERSION:
CLOSED-LID RELATIONSHIP TO CHECK:
DESIGN QUANTITY / DESTINATION:
OPEN QUESTION / RESPONSIBLE PERSON / NEXT ACTION:
ACTUAL SAMPLE ID / DATED OBSERVATION:
DECISION / APPROVER / DATE:

Keep measurements, proposals and actual observations in separate lines. A blank is an open question, not zero.

### Turn a missing input into a specific question

| Entry in your sheet | Question to send | Useful reply to retain
| Earring backs included, projection unknown | Please confirm the complete rear envelope for item E2 with its backs fitted | Item reference, measured envelope and units
| Pendant contact area marked, material open | Please propose the contact material for holder H1 at the marked surfaces | Material identity and proposed contact-review scope
| Chain removal order not decided | Which lift point and chain route are proposed for this arrangement? | Marked drawing or sample view with the intended action
| Box cavity quoted, measurement basis missing | Which drawing and internal reference points does this cavity size describe? | Drawing version, datum and measurement basis
On a small screen, scroll the table sideways to read every field.
The rows are hypothetical examples of question writing. Replace their identifiers with your own references; they are not product specifications or completed sample results.

### Pass the record to the right next task
If you need help choosing the measurement points, use the seven-measurement guide. That article explains the method; this page is the shorter working record to fill and reuse.
When a dimensional field becomes an approval condition, agree its method through the dimension and acceptance worksheet. Keep open-display, closed-pack and removal observations separate in the sample record.
Explore holder directions after describing the product interfaces. The converter’s custom insert overview is a structural reference, while the filled record and agreed project proposal determine your next questions.

## Build a mailer brief suppliers can evaluate

URL: https://www.packrivo.com/guide-mailer-rfq-checklist/

Start with the packed product, not the outside of a box you like. Record dimensions, weight, quantity and destination, then separate confirmed requirements from open questions.

### Measure what actually goes inside
List every item, including pouches, wrapping and cards. Record length, width and height in one unit, together with weight and the intended orientation. Tell the reviewer whether your numbers describe the product, the packed bundle or the box interior. Do not assume these are interchangeable.

### Write the protection requirement
Explain whether an item is fragile, easily scratched, leak-prone or likely to move. Describe the planned parcel journey and any outer carton. Ask for a box and insert proposal together. A concept image cannot prove drop, compression or moisture performance.

### Separate artwork from structure
Specify inside print, outside print or both. State your color expectations and any finish you want explored. Request the final supplier-approved dieline before placing artwork. A dieline for another box size or supplier is a reference, not a production file.

### Make quotations comparable
Use the same specification revision for each quotation. Ask each supplier to identify tooling, sampling, packaging, freight, taxes and exclusions separately. Record the delivery term and named destination. Compare the complete stated scope, not just a unit price.

### Copy this starter briefProduct and packed dimensions (with unit):
Packed weight and item count:
Proposed internal box size, or sizing help needed:
Protection concerns and insert direction:
Inside/outside print and finish:
Expected quantity:
Destination country and postal code:
Target date (request, not a commitment):
Open questions for the supplier:

### Before production approval
Check the assembled sample with the actual contents, approve the artwork revision and agree the transport checks. A digital proof helps review artwork but does not replace physical fit or shipping validation.

### Further reading
- ISTA packaging test procedure overview — context for discussing transport evidence, not a test result for this mailer.
- U.S. International Trade Administration Incoterms overview — context for specifying the delivery term and place in a quotation.
Use these sources to prepare questions. The agreed supplier specification and actual sample or test results take precedence for each project.

Bring the details you have. Mark anything else as unknown.Get a QuoteExplore related packaging →

## Packaging Guides & Buyer Worksheets

URL: https://www.packrivo.com/guides/

### Choose a tool for your next decision
Use these blank worksheets to prepare a project or record a review. Read the method before filling in your own copy; keep measured facts, estimates and open questions distinct.

### 1. Plan the packaging

#### Plan quantities by design
Separate a launch quantity into packaging design lines.InputProduct counts, design differences and component quantities.OutputA quantity plan with the surplus or missing inputs visible.ConfirmationBuyer confirms design counts; the seller confirms each quoted line.
Read the method · Download blank CSV

#### Map approval dependencies
Find which missing input holds the next review back.InputFiles, decision owners, prerequisite inputs and requested dates.OutputAn approval sequence with accepted outputs and open dependencies.ConfirmationNamed reviewers confirm prerequisites and planned dates.
Read the method · Download blank CSV

### 2. Prepare and compare proposals

#### Clarify supplier replies
Keep a proposal separate from the question it still needs to answer.InputYour question, dated supplier reply, files and unresolved scope.OutputA clarification record with an owner and next action.ConfirmationThe responsible buyer and supplier contacts resolve each open row.
Read the method · Download blank CSV

#### Compare flat and assembled delivery
Identify the work that remains when packaging arrives.InputSupplied state, assembly, packing, storage and delivery responsibilities.OutputA comparable description of the completed packaging requirement.ConfirmationBuyer and seller agree the work included in each proposal.
Read the method · Download blank CSV

### 3. Review dimensions, color and samples

#### Agree dimensions and acceptance methods
Give critical dimensions a location and a review method.InputTarget feature, measurement condition, reference and method.OutputA dimension record with project-specific acceptance criteria.ConfirmationThe parties agree the method and the named reviewer checks it.
Read the method · Download blank CSV

#### Record color approval
Keep a surface decision tied to a physical reference.InputReference, material, finish, viewing conditions and sample version.OutputA surface-by-surface color decision with unresolved items visible.ConfirmationThe named approver accepts the reference and the reviewed result.
Read the method · Download blank CSV

#### Track a sample revision
Follow an observed issue through correction and review.InputSample version, issue evidence, requested change and supplier response.OutputA revision record connecting the change to a fresh decision.ConfirmationThe responsible approver reviews the revised evidence before closure.
Read the method · Download blank CSV

### 4. Control changes and acceptance

#### Review a dieline change
Identify which approvals a drawing or artwork change affects.InputDrawing and artwork versions, proposed change and linked components.OutputAn impact record of the files and decisions to review again.ConfirmationNamed owners accept the affected configuration before release.
Read the method · Download blank CSV

#### Close an acceptance issue
Keep a requirement, observation and closing evidence together.InputApproved reference, review method, issue and corrective action.OutputAn acceptance record showing the open or closed decision.ConfirmationThe agreed decision owner accepts the closing evidence.
Read the method · Download blank CSV

### 5. Prepare the shipment

#### Prepare carton and freight data
Describe the actual shipping units for a freight inquiry.InputCarton count, outer dimensions, weights, units and delivery format.OutputA versioned shipment description with estimates identified.ConfirmationPacking and freight contacts confirm the data and quoted scope.
Read the method · Download blank CSV

### Match document requests to the intended use
Use the material-claim evidence record to connect each proposed statement to its component, supporting document and reviewer before artwork approval.
Give the destination and intended use before asking a supplier for documents. If the proposed packaging involves food contact, use the food-contact document checklist to identify the questions for the relevant responsible parties. It is an educational reference, not a Packrivo food-packaging offer or a compliance approval.
For a first message to a supplier, start with the packaging brief essentials. For price comparisons, use the quotation scope checklist alongside the clarification worksheet.

## Custom Molded Pulp Insert Sourcing

URL: https://www.packrivo.com/molded-pulp-insert/

AI-assisted packaging concept. Review the actual materials and fit on a physical sample.

Inserts

## Custom Molded Pulp Insert Sourcing
MOQ: 500 pieces per product / design. Plan the quantity for each design in your brief.
A molded-pulp insert uses formed fiber to create product-shaped cavities. Define product outlines, layout, visible surface and the task the insert must perform.
Packaging option · Supplier confirmation required

### Quote Details
Send the product dimensions, quantity by design and destination. Include the structure, material and artwork preferences you already know; flag open questions so supplier quotes use the same scope.Get a QuoteExplore customization →

### Product specifications
Minimum orderMOQ 500 pieces per design.
Get a QuoteShare your dimensions, quantity and delivery destination.
The minimum is Packrivo’s project threshold. Availability for the chosen structure, material and finish is checked for your project.

### What affects the price
-

#### Size
The finished dimensions and usable interior determine material use and product fit.
-

#### Structure
Closures, folds and assembly steps change the work needed to make the pack.
-

#### Materials
Board, wrap and insert choices affect both construction and material cost.
-

#### Printing & finishes
Artwork coverage, colors and decoration steps change setup and finishing costs.
-

#### Quantity
The quantity per design changes how setup and production costs are distributed.

### When it may fit
It may suit a shaped support concept or a natural-fiber look. Feasibility, tooling and quantity requirements need supplier review before it becomes a public offer.

### Details to define

- Product dimensions, weight, count and orientation.

- Cavity shape, support points and edge access.

- Insert dimensions, surface expectation and box fit.

- Destination market and evidence needed for any fiber or sustainability statement.

### Artwork and presentation choices
Review the proposed formed surface and the surfaces that contact the product. Keep any color or printed-mark proposal tied to a material and process response rather than drawing conclusions from the concept image.

### Options to compare

- Compare natural molded texture with a smoother pulp surface as a sample question.

- Consider colored pulp only if supplier confirms the material and color route.

- Do not infer compostability or recycled content from appearance.

### Choose a full cavity, corner cradle or end-support pair

Specify where formed-fiber support is needed before selecting the overall insert outline. A full cavity and local supports meet different regions of the same product or primary pack; they should not share an unexamined contact drawing.

| Support layout | Identify in the proposal | Review with the complete outer pack

| Full cavity | Product envelope, cavity contour, contact regions, insert seating and removal grip. | Loading, installed position and the customer’s exit route.

| Corner cradles | Each corner position, part orientation, permitted contact and projections or wrap seams to keep clear. | Contact at the product or retail-box corner, the outer-box corner and the starting release grip.

| End-support pair | End A and end B, their contoured parts, surface identities and linked tooling revision. | Both product ends installed in the outer box; keep differently shaped ends individually identified.

Ask for the actual formed-part and material proposal, supplied state and any tooling scope. For a nested delivery, record the empty nesting arrangement and how parts are separated before loading. Empty nesting does not establish the loaded pack’s performance.

Folded corrugated end caps remain a different proposal: their local folds and assembly are not the same as receiving molded-fiber parts. Compare both against the same allowed contact regions without assuming identical manufacturing or support behavior.

### Supplier confirmation needed
Confirm composition, forming process, tooling, sample and commercial conditions with the supplier. Any recycled, recyclable, compostable or protection claim needs product-specific evidence.

### Project details to agree
Final materials, construction, price, sample scope and delivery timing are confirmed for the agreed project with the relevant supplier.

### Frequently asked questionsDoes molded pulp automatically mean compostable?No. Composition, coatings, additives and local conditions matter. Verify evidence for the specific insert and market before making a claim.Is the material, supplier or performance confirmed?No. This is an inquiry candidate. Material specifications, supplier feasibility, project price and timing and performance need project-specific evidence.

### Request a Quote
Compare with Packrivo packaging options and Get a Quote. This local page draft does not create a commercial offer.

## Arka vs Packrivo: Custom Packaging Compared

URL: https://www.packrivo.com/packrivo-vs-arka/

### At a glance

|
| Arka
| Packrivo

| What it is
| An online platform for branded mailers, shippers and eco-friendly packaging
| A Shenzhen-based sourcing and project coordination service working with independent packaging factories

| Minimum order
| Branded mailers: 1 sample, then production from 10 units; custom cartons 250 units
| 500 pieces per design

| Production time
| Standard 7–10 business days, rush 3–5 business days, after proof approval
| Stock jewelry boxes 1–15 days; custom printed boxes about 5–30 days after approval

| Pricing
| Shown while you configure online
| Itemised supplier quotation plus an 8–15% coordination fee, with the calculation basis agreed in writing

| Best for
| Very small branded mailer runs and fast standard production
| Small jewelry, candle and gift brands ordering 500+ custom boxes per design

### When Arka is the better fit

- You need branded mailer or shipper boxes in quantities from 10 units.

- Speed matters most and a 3–10 business-day production window fits your launch.

- You prefer ordering through an online configurator.

### When Packrivo is the better fit

- Your packaging is a jewelry box with an insert rather than a shipping mailer.

- You want factory quotations compared at 500+ pieces per design, where unit costs fall.

- You need finishes such as hot foil, debossing, velvet inserts or textured wrap paper on rigid boxes.

### How to decide

- Count the pieces you need per design. Under 500, start with an online platform; at 500 or more, compare factory quotations.

- Name the structure: mailer, folding carton, drawer box, magnetic box or collapsible gift box. Rigid jewelry boxes are where factory sourcing differs most.

- List the finishes you need (foil, debossing, inserts) and ask every supplier to quote the same specification.

- Compare delivered cost, including freight and duties, not only the unit price.

### Frequently asked questions

#### Is Packrivo cheaper than Arka?

It depends on quantity and box type. Arka suits small runs ordered online. Packrivo works from 500 pieces per design, where factory unit prices fall; for example, stock drawer jewelry boxes start around US$0.10–0.33 per box ex-works before logo printing, freight and duties, plus an 8–15% coordination fee, with the calculation basis agreed in writing.

#### What is the minimum order at Packrivo compared with Arka?

Packrivo projects start at 500 pieces per design. Arka: Branded mailers: 1 sample, then production from 10 units; custom cartons 250 units.

#### How long does production take?

Arka: Standard 7–10 business days, rush 3–5 business days, after proof approval. At Packrivo, stock jewelry box styles typically ship in 1–15 days and custom printed gift boxes or mailers take about 5–30 days after artwork and sample approval, plus international shipping.

### Sources

Supplier terms were checked on their own pages on October 22, 2026. Terms change; confirm current minimums, prices and timing with each supplier before ordering.

- Arka FAQ

Get a quote from Packrivo See all comparisons

## Privacy & Inquiry Data

URL: https://www.packrivo.com/privacy/

### What happens to your packaging request?
The brief builder prepares a summary in your browser. Selecting Send My Packaging Request transmits the request to Packrivo. A reference ID confirms that it has been saved. If the confirmation is lost or the status is uncertain, retry the same details; the website checks for the existing request to avoid creating a duplicate. We store your packaging option, approximate dimensions, quantity, destination country, work email, any optional details you supply, and the source page so an authorized person can review and respond. We use this information for inquiry handling, not automatic marketing subscription. The request is scheduled for deletion after 180 days. You can request earlier deletion or ask about your data at [email protected]; include the request ID if you have it. Copying and downloading save data to your device; WhatsApp and email open only when you choose them.

### Optional Inquiry Attachments
You may attach up to 3 files to an inquiry: no more than 2 MiB per file and 5 MiB in total. Attachments are stored privately with the inquiry and can be read only by authorized administrators. They expire with the inquiry under the same 180-day retention schedule, including earlier deletion when requested. Do not upload confidential customer information or files you are not authorized to share.

### Retention, backups and notifications
The 180-day period applies to your submitted request and linked working records. Scheduled checks remove expired records. Routine website backups exclude request tables; restricted request snapshots are checked for expiry and recorded deletion requests before they can be restored. If you request earlier deletion, a restricted reference prevents an old backup from reintroducing your request.
Administrative email notifications contain a request reference and a link to the private inbox, rather than the details you submitted. Only authorized administrators can review requests. Messages you choose to send directly by email or WhatsApp are handled separately; contact Packrivo if you want to request deletion. Technical logs have the shorter retention periods below.

### Analytics
Packrivo uses Google Analytics 4 through Google Tag to understand how visitors use this site. The tag measures page views and configured interactions. When you choose a WhatsApp link, an event records the page path, link label, destination and contact button position; it does not include the values entered in your brief. Google Analytics may use first-party cookies or similar browser identifiers. Data retention follows the setting in the connected Google Analytics property. See Google’s Privacy Policy.

### Technical logs
For site operation and security, the web server records technical request details such as IP address, requested path, time, referring page and browser information. Access logs rotate daily and are kept for up to 52 daily rotations. PHP error logs are kept for up to 14 daily rotations.

### Email and WhatsApp
Email and WhatsApp open only when you choose those links. These services handle messages you send under their own privacy terms. Your packaging brief is not attached to a WhatsApp or email link automatically; review and share only what you choose.
Prefer email? [email protected]
Opens your email application. Review and send your message yourself; no form data is attached automatically.

## Packaging Sourcing & Project Coordination

URL: https://www.packrivo.com/services/

PACKAGING SOURCING & PROJECT COORDINATION

## Packaging Sourcing Services
Shenzhen-based Packrivo helps you source packaging with independent packaging suppliers, from product specifications and quotation comparison to sample review. MOQ: 500 pieces per product/design.Get a Quote
AI-assisted concept. Not a physical sample or production proof.

### Service Fees & Project Scope
Our service fee range is 8–15%. Agree the fee calculation basis, included work, any exceptions and payment terms in writing for your project before paid work starts. The percentage alone is not a complete quotation.
MOQ: 500 pieces per product/design. Packaging materials, construction, sampling, production price and delivery timing are confirmed for the agreed specification with the relevant supplier.

### From brief to shipping.
Agree the scope, price and responsible parties before each paid stage.
- 1

#### Brief
You + Packrivo
Share product dimensions, quantity per design, artwork and destination.
- 2

#### Supplier matching
Packrivo + Supplier
Check structure, materials, quantity and the supplier’s proposed scope.
- 3

#### Quotation
Supplier + Packrivo + You
Compare itemized terms and approve the commercial scope.
- 4

#### Sample
Supplier + You
Make and review the agreed sample; record changes and approval.
- 5

#### Production & inspection
Supplier + Packrivo + You
Produce to the approved specification; agree inspection scope and release responsibility.
- 6

#### Shipping
Supplier / logistics provider + You
Confirm packed dimensions, delivery terms and destination requirements.

### Before a supplier discussion
Review the brief, the intended recipient and every detail you are willing to share. Do not include confidential artwork or sensitive customer files at this stage.

### Before an order or payment
Obtain the seller identity and written project terms. Confirm the specification, quantity, currency, sample or proof scope, delivery assumptions, schedule, approvals and issue handling directly with the relevant seller. A brief is not a quote, order or supplier approval.

### Agree who confirms each decision

#### Your brand
Supply the product facts and intended use. Decide what may be shared, which proposal to pursue and who can approve the sample.

#### Packrivo
Organize the brief, supplier questions, comparison and review records around the agreed project. Keep open decisions and their owners visible.

#### The relevant supplier or seller
Confirm the proposed construction, materials, quotation, sample scope and delivery terms for its offer. The written offer identifies the contracting seller.
Before paid work, sampling or an order, agree the responsible parties, deliverables, any charges, payment terms and approval points in writing. Use the project worksheets to prepare the questions; the worksheet itself does not approve a quotation or order.

### Start with the details you have.
Mark the rest as open questions for review.Get a Quote

## Terms of Service

URL: https://www.packrivo.com/terms/

### Who we are

Packrivo provides packaging sourcing and project coordination from Shenzhen, China, for small jewelry, candle and gift brands. Independent suppliers manufacture the packaging. Each project quotation names the contracting seller, the scope of our coordination and how our fee is calculated.

### Website information

Product pages, guides and articles are general information to help you prepare a packaging brief. They are not legal, regulatory or compliance advice. Images labelled concept render illustrate packaging options; they are not physical samples or completed customer projects.

### Quotations and orders

Prices, materials, minimum order quantities and timings on this website are indicative. A project becomes binding only when you accept a written quotation. The quotation sets out the supplier costs, our coordination scope and fee basis, sample terms, payment stages and delivery terms. If a quotation differs from these terms, the quotation applies to that project.

### Samples and approval

Production follows the sample or specification you approve in writing. Changes requested after approval may affect cost, timing or both, and are confirmed before work continues.

### Your artwork and information

You confirm that you have the right to use the logos, artwork and product information you send us. We use them only to prepare and deliver your project and share them only with the suppliers and service providers involved in it.

### Inspection, shipping and risk

The inspection scope, packed dimensions, delivery terms and the point at which risk passes to you are agreed in the quotation for each project.

### Liability

To the extent permitted by law, our responsibility for a project is limited to the coordination services described in its quotation. Nothing in these terms limits rights you have under laws that cannot be excluded.

### Privacy

How we handle packaging requests, attachments, analytics and technical logs is described in Privacy & inquiry data.

### Changes and contact

We may update these terms; the date above shows the latest version. Questions about these terms can be sent to [email protected].

## Custom Thermoformed Insert Tray Sourcing
MOQ: 500 pieces per product / design. Plan the quantity for each design in your brief.
A thermoformed insert tray uses a formed plastic sheet to create cavities around products. PET is one candidate material option for this visual, not a supplier-confirmed resin. Specify the items, cavity layout, tray dimensions and how the tray sits inside the outer box.
Packaging option · Supplier confirmation required

### Quote Details
Send the product dimensions, quantity by design and destination. Include the structure, material and artwork preferences you already know; flag open questions so supplier quotes use the same scope.Get a QuoteExplore customization →

### Product specifications
Minimum orderMOQ 500 pieces per design.
Get a QuoteShare your dimensions, quantity and delivery destination.
The minimum is Packrivo’s project threshold. Availability for the chosen structure, material and finish is checked for your project.

### What affects the price
-

#### Size
The finished dimensions and usable interior determine material use and product fit.
-

#### Structure
Closures, folds and assembly steps change the work needed to make the pack.
-

#### Materials
Board, wrap and insert choices affect both construction and material cost.
-

#### Printing & finishes
Artwork coverage, colors and decoration steps change setup and finishing costs.
-

#### Quantity
The quantity per design changes how setup and production costs are distributed.

### When it may fit
It may suit a clear or shaped presentation layout. It may not suit a buyer who requires a fiber-only package or a market-specific material claim without documentation.

### Details to define

- Product dimensions, number, weight and orientation.

- Cavity shape, depth, access and tray outer dimensions.

- Resin, color/clarity and any paperboard carrier or sleeve.

- Destination market, product-contact requirements and sample criteria.

### Artwork and presentation choices
Give each cavity a content reference and show where the backing-card artwork remains visible. Identify any proposed print or label separately from the formed tray material.

### Options to compare

- Compare a transparent tray with colored or opaque options only after material confirmation.

- Use a graphic card behind cavities to add color without changing the tray.

- Separate visual presentation from retention or protection claims.

### Record cavity layout, empty nesting and loaded stacking separately

Identify the complete gift components before drawing a multi-cavity insert. Give every item a cavity ID, packing orientation and removal area, including its primary pack, cap, strap or other projection.

| State or decision | Proposal fields | Review question

| Loaded multi-cavity layout | Cavity-to-item map, walls between adjacent cavities, loading order and removal grip. | Can each item be loaded and removed with the other planned components present?

| Empty nesting | Empty orientation, locating surfaces, supplied stack and separation grip. | How does the operator separate the delivered trays before packing?

| Loaded stacking, if proposed | Loaded orientation, support ledges, upper-tray contact and clearance above the actual contents. | What supports the next tray, and what touches or clears the product beneath it?

Request separate empty and loaded views for a nest-and-stack proposal. A changed orientation may be part of an offered design; it is not a universal feature of formed trays. Do not infer loaded stackability from compact empty nesting.

Keep the offered sheet, resin, forming and rim details with the cavity drawing. These choices describe a box insert; they do not by themselves define a hinged outer clamshell, a sealed blister, a universal material grade or a verified load rating.

### Supplier confirmation needed
Confirm resin, wall thickness, forming method, cut edges, fit and market documentation with supplier. No recyclability, contact-safety or performance claim is made here.

### Project details to agree
Final materials, construction, price, sample scope and delivery timing are confirmed for the agreed project with the relevant supplier.

### Frequently asked questionsCan a thermoformed tray fit a product with a custom shape?A supplier may be able to review a formed cavity, but dimensions, draft, resin and tooling must be checked for the actual item.Is the material, supplier or performance confirmed?No. This is an inquiry candidate. Material specifications, supplier feasibility, project price and timing and performance need project-specific evidence.

## Custom Auto Bottom Display Carton Sourcing

URL: https://www.packrivo.com/product-auto-bottom-display-carton/

PACKAGING OPTION

## Custom Auto Bottom Display Carton Sourcing
Review an auto bottom display carton by watching the prepared base form before retail units are loaded. Then assess the front opening in full and half-full states. Automatic bottom formation describes a setup feature; it doesn't confirm the finished display's capacity or suitability for your particular contents.
AI-assisted concept illustration. Separate prepared base setup from the retail access and refill task.

### Product specifications
Minimum orderMOQ 500 pieces per design.
Get a QuoteShare your dimensions, quantity and delivery destination.
The minimum is Packrivo’s project threshold. Availability for the chosen structure, material and finish is checked for your project.

### What affects the price
-

#### Size
The finished dimensions and usable interior determine material use and product fit.
-

#### Structure
Closures, folds and assembly steps change the work needed to make the pack.
-

#### Materials
Board, wrap and insert choices affect both construction and material cost.
-

#### Printing & finishes
Artwork coverage, colors and decoration steps change setup and finishing costs.
-

#### Quantity
The quantity per design changes how setup and production costs are distributed.

This packaging option combines a display opening with a prepared base. Keep those two jobs separate in the drawing and sample record. Staff form the carton; customers remove units later under a different load arrangement.

### Show the prepared base in motion
The auto bottom display catalogue describes pre-glued flaps that form the bottom. Request the supplier’s base drawing and setup demonstration for the proposed version. Do not infer the actual joints from a closed display photograph.
Ask for a sample in its delivered state. Open the carton as instructed and view the base before placing anything inside. Photograph the final base position from below and from the inside, where safe and practical.
Record any separate pressing or alignment step. If the proposed preparation changes, update the packing instruction rather than continuing to call two different operations the same setup.

### Use the retail units as a defined load
Provide the number, dimensions and weight of the actual sale-ready units. If their weight is unknown, mark it as missing. A display filled with empty dummy cartons can help review layout, but it cannot stand in for a loaded-base evaluation.
A hypothetical tray of small gift boxes may be full when placed on a counter and half full later. Check the front cutout and remaining rows in both states. Staff should know whether units are moved forwards or remain in their original positions.
The four corner display tray focuses on prepared corners and front access. Compare it here by setup action and proposed base construction, without assigning a strength ranking from the format name.

### Record base setup and display use separately
Start each comparison with the same sample revision and unit configuration. Keep setup observations apart from access observations.

| Stage | Required view | Record
| Supplied | Prepared flat structure | Which joints arrive completed
| Formed empty | Bottom inside and underneath | Final flap positions and extra actions
| Full display | Front and side with actual units | Base shape and front-row grip
| Half full | Same views with fewer units | Remaining layout and removal action
If the base moves during a removal action, record when and where. A description such as ‘center flap moved while front unit was lifted’ is more useful than a general request for a stronger box.

### Specify the supplied state and evaluation scope
Confirm who sets up the carton, loads units and prepares any display cutout. Agree whether inserts or dividers are separate pieces. The delivery-state guide helps name those responsibilities.
Board grade, caliper, flute where relevant and base preparation belong in the proposal. Ask the responsible supplier to define any required load or handling evaluation. The fit and access record on this page is an editorial review method, not a test standard.
Check printing after the carton is formed. Any display panel that changes position during setup should be reviewed in its final retail state before artwork approval.
Keep any partial-load arrangement deliberate. ‘Half full’ should describe which rows remain, not simply half the initial unit count placed wherever convenient. Photograph that starting pattern and ask whether staff must move units forwards. This gives the base and access review one repeatable configuration without turning a casual trial into a capacity claim.

### Send setup evidence with the unit layout
Use Get a Quote to send supplied and formed views, actual unit details, display layout, quantity and destination. The minimum is 500 pieces per design. Final components, sample work, price and timing require project confirmation.

### Request a Quote
Packrivo’s minimum is 500 pieces per design. Confirm the configuration, supplier proposal, sample scope and commercial terms for your project.Get a Quote

## Custom Auto Bottom Slotted Outer Carton Sourcing
Specify an auto bottom slotted outer carton by its delivered preparation, formed base and separate top-sealing work. Ask for the actual base proposal, then review setup before loading the complete contents. The structure name doesn't settle what the packing operator still needs to do.
AI-assisted concept illustration. Separate supplied base preparation, local setup and top sealing in the packing scope.

### Product specifications
Minimum orderMOQ 500 pieces per design.
Get a QuoteShare your dimensions, quantity and delivery destination.
The minimum is Packrivo’s project threshold. Availability for the chosen structure, material and finish is checked for your project.

### What affects the price
-

#### Size
The finished dimensions and usable interior determine material use and product fit.
-

#### Structure
Closures, folds and assembly steps change the work needed to make the pack.
-

#### Materials
Board, wrap and insert choices affect both construction and material cost.
-

#### Printing & finishes
Artwork coverage, colors and decoration steps change setup and finishing costs.
-

#### Quantity
The quantity per design changes how setup and production costs are distributed.

Keep this inquiry concept tied to one proposed carton drawing. The supplier must confirm its joints, material and preparation rather than leaving those details to a closed outer-pack image.

### Obtain the supplied and formed base views

The auto bottom slotted reference establishes the named format. Request a drawing or sample demonstration that shows the base before setup and after it reaches its intended position. Identify any joints completed before delivery.

Ask the supplier to state the remaining operator actions. If staff must press a flap, align a joint or inspect a feature, include that step before loading. Record the actual instruction instead of promising a universal assembly speed.

A snap bottom outer carton uses a manual-base proposal to compare. Keep the comparison on delivered work and the confirmed forming sequence; do not rank the formats by promotional strength language.

### Load the complete outer-pack configuration

Provide the count, packed dimensions and actual weight of all retail units. Include dividers, padding and documents. If a weight remains unknown, leave a labeled gap rather than estimating a convenient load.

For a hypothetical outer pack of gift boxes, form and inspect the base first. Place the units in their proposed rows, then add the remaining components. A trial filled with empty dummies can clarify arrangement while leaving the actual loaded review open.

Record the top-closing order and the available sealing method at the packing location. Staff may receive a prepared base but still perform all top sealing. Those operations should have different owners or checkpoints if the actual process needs them.

### Use a base-to-seal handoff table

Keep the base drawing, loading plan and top instruction at the same revision. One row should describe one stage that can be observed.

| Stage | Delivered or completed state | Responsibility and evidence
| Delivery | Prepared joints identified | Supplier view of actual supplied form
| Setup | Base in drawn position | Operator sequence and empty-base photo
| Loading | Complete rows and inner components | Packer and loaded top view
| Top seal | Flaps closed by agreed method | Operator, tool and seam photo
| Receiving | Intended access to contents | Opening route and packed reference

The delivery-state guide helps keep prepared parts separate from remaining work. Ask whether any additional tape or joining operation is part of the proposal, and retain that answer in the table.

### Keep sample observations within their scope

Inspect the base after the actual contents are inserted, then inspect the final top seams. Name any visible movement by part and stage. A tidy empty base doesn’t establish a capacity for the loaded set.

Agree any required handling or distribution evaluation with the responsible party. The setup record can supply the configuration and process context, while the evaluation needs its own method and evidence.

### Send the remaining-work brief

Submit the supplied and formed base views, complete load, top-sealing request, quantity and destination through Get a Quote. Packrivo’s minimum is 500 pieces per design. Final construction, sample work and commercial terms are agreed for the actual project.

## Custom Auto-Lock Bottom Carton Sourcing
An auto-lock bottom carton uses a prepared bottom arrangement that opens into position as the carton is formed. Consider this option when a packing process needs to set up a retail carton before loading a candle vessel or small gift. The useful brief explains how the base opens, how the contents bear on it and what the packing team must verify before closing the top.

### Describe the prepared bottom

Ask for the actual bottom drawing and supplied state. A prepared glued arrangement differs from loose flaps that an operator folds and locks by hand. The name alone should not decide what arrives: confirm which joints are already made, which folds move during setup and what a correctly opened base looks like.

Keep the bottom decision separate from the top closure. A tuck top, lock detail or other proposed top can change loading and opening without changing the base principle. Define where the product enters, which end the customer opens and whether a card or insert must be loaded before the product.

For a candle jar, record the complete vessel weight and base shape, including the lid. The bottom review needs the intended loaded configuration. If a holder spreads or relocates contact, show that component in the same section or layout drawing instead of treating it as an optional detail added after the carton is chosen.

### Choose by the packing task

The existing tuck-end carton option covers an end-flap closure. An auto-lock bottom adds a particular setup and base task that needs its own evidence. Compare proposals by the steps left to the packing team, the proposed material and the review of the loaded base, rather than assuming a universal speed or load advantage.

A supplier demonstration can explain the intended movement. Then have someone follow the proposed steps with a representative sample. Record an awkward action, an incompletely opened corner or a product that changes the bottom’s position as a specific observation. This gives the next discussion an answerable issue.

### Send a base and loading brief

| Field | Required detail

| Product | Overall dimensions, complete weight, base contact and orientation.

| Carton | Formed interior, top closure and approved bottom construction.

| Supply | Prepared joints, remaining folds and packing instructions.

| Components | Holder, tissue or card and the order in which each is loaded.

| Artwork | Print panels, seam and folds linked to a named dieline.

| Quantity | Units by design, destination and packing or sample scope.

If the product has a heavy or uneven base, identify the support question for the supplier instead of inserting a guessed material grade. The complete candle packaging brief separates the presentation carton, holder and outer parcel pack so their tasks stay clear.

### Review setup before approving the appearance

- Open the carton from its actual supplied state.
- Inspect the prepared joints and whether the base reaches the proposed position.
- Load the complete product through the intended end.
- Check that the holder and contents leave room for the top closure.
- Review the agreed handling task with the named sample and record observations.
- Inspect print alignment and folds after the structure has been formed.

A changed base or seam can affect the dieline used for artwork. Keep a structural revision connected to the print file, and repeat the affected review rather than carrying forward an approval for a different configuration.

### Agree the scope for this design

Packrivo’s order planning policy is 500 pieces per product / design. List the carton, prepared bottom work, additional parts and sampling in the proposal. The final material, price, production conditions and schedule are confirmed for that configuration.

A manufacturer’s carton-style reference identifies auto-bottom and snap-lock choices as different structures. Use it for naming the proposal, not for transferring supply or performance claims.

Get a Quote with the product’s base, weight and loading sequence. Packrivo helps connect those inputs to supplier questions and a sample review with a clear decision.

### Start with the packed thickness

Measure the product in the form that will be wrapped. A protective cover, print bundle, card or additional paper layer can change its thickness and the surfaces it presents to the mailer. If several configurations are planned, list them separately rather than describing them all as one flat item.

Ask for the fold pattern and the route taken by each panel. The proposal should show where the product sits, which panels cover its faces and how the edges are enclosed. Any selectable fold position needs an explanation of the intended range and how the packing team chooses it.

Locate the closing joint after the wrap has been formed. The tape or proposed sealing component belongs in the specification, along with the surface available for the shipping label. A rendered exterior should not substitute for a drawing of the loaded wrap and its closing work.

### Compare wrapping with loading a box

A front-tuck mailer is loaded into a formed box with its own closing flaps. A book-wrap option forms around the contents, which changes the packing order and thickness discussion. Compare those actions and the product geometry rather than assuming a wrap is suitable for every thin gift.

For an illustrative notebook-and-card set, the loose card may need to be placed inside a cover or otherwise located before wrapping. A loose item that slides into the closing seam creates another review question. Record that arrangement instead of adding a generic instruction to use more packing material.

### Brief the folds and closing route

| Input | What to record

| Product | Length, width, complete packed thickness, weight and sensitive corners.

| Arrangement | Covers, bundles, cards and the position of every loose component.

| Wrap | Proposed fold pattern, panel coverage and edge enclosure.

| Closure | Joint location, sealing supplies and the customer opening route.

| Files | Artwork, shipping information area and named construction drawing.

| Scope | Design quantities, destination, supplied state and review requirements.

For changing product thickness, identify the configurations the sample review must cover. The measurement method worksheet helps name where thickness is measured and which condition applies. A nominal product size may omit a cover, projecting corner or packing layer.

### Review wrap formation and access

- Prepare the complete product arrangement.
- Place it at the proposed location on the flat wrap.
- Fold the panels in sequence and inspect the edges and corners.
- Seal the joint using the intended method.
- Open the package and remove the contents by the intended customer action.
- Define a route-specific transport review for the complete pack.

Record a corner gap, unexpected crease or difficult opening as a feature-level issue. If a different fold position or closing material is proposed, review the resulting pack again. Changes should remain connected to the thickness configuration that prompted them.

### Prepare the proposal without assumed savings

Packrivo’s order planning policy is 500 pieces per product / design. Identify the wrap, sealing component, printed extras and sample work in the scope. Actual carton data and the selected route determine the freight discussion; final construction, price, production conditions and delivery timing are confirmed for the project.

The FEFCO construction reference provides a shared way to identify folded corrugated designs. Have the supplier identify the proposed wrap drawing, fold positions and closing method, then review that configuration with the complete contents.

Get a Quote with the thickness configurations and closing route. Packrivo helps clarify the wrap proposal and the sample questions that matter for your flat gift.

### When it may fit

### Compare a grid divider with a folded mailer inlay

A grid and an inlay can separate the same contents while creating different forming and loading tasks. Request their actual part layouts against the same finished outer-box interior.

| Proposal | Identify on the drawing | Keep in the loading review

| Grid divider | Each strip, slot, cell, installed height and the item assigned to that cell. | Strip engagement, installed orientation, loading order and removal of each item.

| Folded mailer inlay | Fold panels, tabs, compartments, retaining features and contact with the selected mailer. | Forming and installation before loading; clearance for wrap, cards and accessories when the mailer closes.

Record the complete packed dimensions and weight of every item, the outer-box drawing and the parts delivered. State who forms and installs the inlay. Compare the installed arrangement with the same products rather than deciding from a flat insert outline or compartment count alone.

Keep the corrugated grade and flute direction attached to the offered layout. Loaded fit and customer access are separate questions from any claimed transport protection; request the evidence appropriate to each.

### Supplier confirmation needed

### Project details to agree

Final materials, construction, price, sample scope and delivery timing are confirmed for the agreed project with the relevant supplier.

### Frequently asked questions

Can one divider layout hold products of different sizes?

Possibly, but list each item and orientation so the supplier can review the compartments and clearance.

Is the material, supplier or performance confirmed?

No. This is an inquiry candidate. Material specifications, supplier feasibility, project price and timing and performance need project-specific evidence.

### What affects the price

#### Size
The finished dimensions and usable interior determine material use and product fit.
-

#### Structure
Closures, folds and assembly steps change the work needed to make the pack.

#### Materials
Board, wrap and insert choices affect both construction and material cost.
-

#### Printing & finishes
Artwork coverage, colors and decoration steps change setup and finishing costs.

#### Quantity
The quantity per design changes how setup and production costs are distributed.

This packaging concept prepares a supplier discussion. The proposed board construction, fold pattern and closing method need a drawing and an identified material sample.

### Locate the corrugated section on the fold map

The corrugated gusset folder catalogue identifies the format. Use it as a starting option, then request the supplier’s actual board section, gusset positions and joining proposal. The catalog name does not provide your project-specific crease recipe or closure allowance.

Mark the flute direction on the blank beside each named gusset fold. Ask which folds run along that direction and which cross it. Keep the proposed crease treatment with that map so the sample reviewer can identify the actual material and forming action rather than comparing two unexplained folded shapes.

Name the complete thinner and thicker bundles. Include backing, wrap and local high points. For a hypothetical print gift, the thicker bundle adds a booklet; mark its spine beside the relevant gusset instead of recording only a maximum stack thickness.

### Separate fit from the outer-pack closing job

The paperboard gusset folder tool asks how a contents stack meets side folds and a lock. Here, the packing instruction must also identify the corrugated section at those folds, stacked board layers around the edges and the surface available for the outer-pack joint. Those fields can change which panel is formed first, where an edge is enclosed and where the packer applies the agreed closure.

Confirm the actual outer-pack closing method before carrying over a paperboard folder’s packing action. A gusset accommodates the bundle; the specified outer-pack joint closes it. Mark them separately, even when the same panel participates in both actions.

### Complete a corrugated fold-and-joint map

Use one drawing revision and actual material identity throughout the record. Replace the example references with your own marked views.

| Relationship | Input to mark | Sample evidence

| Flute direction at gussets | Direction arrow and fold IDs G1/G2 | Flat blank and formed views showing the same folds

| Board build-up | Overlapping layers beside each bundle edge | Located side section at the booklet spine and corners

| Edge enclosure | Which panel covers each packed edge; exposed cut edges | Loaded views before final joining

| Joining or seal surface | Actual method, receiving face and overlap proposed | Surface available after each named fill is wrapped

| Delivered state | Flat or prepared folds; completed seams and loose parts | Received configuration beside the forming instruction

| Bundle comparison | Complete thin/thick contents and high-point positions | Repeat fold, edge and closing views without changing orientation

For the hypothetical booklet bundle, call the spine edge E1 and closing face J1. If forming the adjacent gusset changes the overlap at J1, ask for that relationship to be reviewed on the drawing. Pulling the folder tighter without recording the action would leave the instruction incomplete.

### Check the actual folding and joining sequence

Start with the sample as delivered. Identify every prepared seam and the work left to the packer. Match the flute arrow and fold IDs to the sample before forming the gussets. Record any visible fold or liner change at the named location; leave the cause for the responsible structural review.

Load each bundle and inspect the board build-up and enclosure at the same edges. Photograph those locations while the final joint is still open. Then record how the proposed closing surface is reached and joined, followed by the customer’s release and removal route.

Keep these observations separate from a transit or protection evaluation. Compare other outer-pack options if a fixed cavity or another packing process is needed.

### Send the fold map with the packed edges

The quantity per design changes how setup and production costs are distributed.

Concept / packaging option. AI-assisted visuals explain the structure; final materials, production details and sample scope are agreed for the project.

### Describe both states of the carton

The supplied pack and the open display may have different roles. Ask whether a cover, flap or separate shipping component is removed, folded back or retained. Show the intended display state with the contents visible. If a rear panel becomes a header, mark its fold and final position.

Record how many retail items the display holds and how they are arranged. Their dimensions should include individual cartons, cards or pouches that remain around the products. The height of the front wall affects what a customer can read and where they can grip an item.

Specify who removes any outer protection before display, and show where that removed material goes during the retailer’s setup.

Think about the layout after some items have been removed. A display that looks orderly when full may leave a different access problem near the last row. Specify whether the retailer refills it, how stock is placed and whether a divider or another holder remains during use.

### Compare a display with an individual gift box

A folding carton for one item usually focuses on its own loading and opening. A counter display adds group arrangement, retailer setup and repeated removal. It may still contain individually boxed products, so those units should remain separate lines in the component list.

For an illustrative group of jewelry pouches on mounting cards, the card tops might carry product information while the display carries brand or price information. Specify which copy belongs to the individual pack and which belongs to the display. That keeps later artwork changes from being sent to the wrong component.

### Write the retailer setup brief

| Field | Describe the display task

| Retail units | Complete unit dimensions, quantity, weight and arrangement.

| Display | Formed footprint, wall heights, header and compartments.

| Setup | What the retailer removes, folds, locks or loads.

| Use | Customer grip, removal order and proposed refill process.

| Files | Copy and artwork linked to the relevant display or retail-unit drawing.

| Delivery | Supplied state, outer pack, destination and sample questions.

Keep the display-unit quantity separate from the number of items sold. A launch with several hundred retail packs does not necessarily need the same number of display cartons. Build the component counts before comparing a combined proposal.

### Review setup, use and replenishment

- Start with the sample in the actual proposed delivery state.
- Complete the retailer setup using the intended instructions.
- Load the full group and inspect the footprint and information panels.
- Remove items from the front and later rows, checking access.
- Review the partly filled arrangement and the proposed refill task.
- Record the display version and any affected retail-pack changes.

If the header, front cutout or compartments change, review how that alters both the flat drawing and the loaded layout. The dieline change record helps identify the decisions that need to be reopened rather than assuming an earlier approval covers the new display.

### Agree the quantity and display scope

Packrivo’s order planning policy is 500 pieces per product / design. Identify the proposed display and its retail-unit quantity basis in the brief, then review project feasibility, sample work, pricing and delivery conditions with the relevant parties.

The original display-box structural reference illustrates the group-presentation format. It does not establish transport or display performance for the configuration you propose.

Get a Quote with the retail-unit arrangement and counter setup. Packrivo helps connect those tasks to a comparable supplier response and a sample review.

### Mark the panels that become the handle
The cube carrier reference identifies a carton carrier with a handle. Request the flat blank and formed view so you can locate the panels that create the grip. Mark each fold, aperture and overlapping panel on the drawing. Handle visibility in a photograph is only a starting point for that identification.

### Use an integral-handle loading record
This hypothetical candle-gift example records questions without assigning a carrying rating.

| Interface | Example input | Sample decision

| Tissue position | Tissue ends remain below handle movement | Observe catching or obstruction
| Carry and set-down | Complete gift mass and intended use | Agree the required handling review

### Locate the hole against the finished bag

The die-cut bag reference identifies a paper bag with a handle design. Request the actual hole outline and its relationship to the top edge, folds and any reinforcement. Keep those layers separate on the proposal.

### Show the highest packed point

### Use a grip-hole relationship record

| Relationship | Input | Observation
| Hole to top edge | Confirmed outline and position | Starting hand entry
| Hole to reinforcement | Named proposed layers | Inside/outside handle view
| Hole to contents | Highest packed point | Available grip space
| Main opening to product | Full gift envelope | Loading and removal route
| Handoff | User support and actual load | Agreed sample action

### Review a located grip question

In the hypothetical map, call the grip hole GH1 and the tissue high point TP1. If TP1 reaches the hand path, ask the supplier to review GH1 against that full configuration. A generic request for a larger bag loses the specific relationship.

If the proposal changes hole position, reinforcement or loaded arrangement, record the changed item and repeat the relevant action. An unchanged outside print file doesn’t guarantee an unchanged grip interface.

Any capacity, repeated-use or handling requirement must be assessed separately for the actual construction and conditions. The map can define the sample configuration but doesn’t establish performance.

Can I use my own branding?

Can you make exactly what the picture shows?

What happens after an inquiry?

A person reviews the brief and missing details. Supplier checks and sample or quotation discussions follow only after the next steps are agreed.
